- **worktree**: worktree 是另一份 checkout（`.claude/worktrees/&lt;名字&gt;/`）：被追蹤的檔案都在，**`.gitignore` 擋掉的一個都沒有**。 下面這幾樣刻意不進版控，卻是做事要用的。少了的時候有的會大聲壞，有的**安靜地少做一件事** —— 後者最危險（2026-11-05 查的）。 ([b09901017/baobao-wellness-clinic-scheduler](https://github.com/b09901017/baobao-wellness-clinic-scheduler/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree.md))
- **worktree**: Creates the isolated worktree for a /devteam:implement run — runs the repo's worktree skill (or git worktree add when the repo names none) with the capabilities the repo names, runs the repo's setup commands, and checks the services the repo lists. Returns only the path, branch, claimed resources and whether setup passed. ([dgigafox/devteam](https://github.com/dgigafox/devteam/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree.md))
- **Worktree Setup**: Use when starting work on any non-trivial task that involves edits, multiple parallel tasks on the same repo, or risk of clobbering another agent/session. Enforces the 'one agent per worktree' rule and produces the wt commands to safely isolate work. ([JaydipGabani/agentkit](https://github.com/JaydipGabani/agentkit/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree-setup.agent.md))
- **worktree-agent**: Use when a subagent should create, manage or clean up git worktrees for parallel work (at most 3 active). Runs the worktree skill's steps. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/agents/worktree-agent.md) · ★1)
- **worktree-agent**: You are a parallel sprint executor in the ADP pipeline. You work in an isolated git worktree on a dedicated branch (`adp/sprint-N`). You implement exactly one task from `tasks.md`, run sensors, and return a result. ([0xPuncker/adp](https://github.com/0xPuncker/adp/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/worktree-agent.md))
- **worktree-agent**: You are a specialized agent for managing git worktrees. Your job is to create, list, and manage worktrees for parallel development. ([launchapp-dev/worktree-manager](https://github.com/launchapp-dev/worktree-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/worktree-agent.md))
- **worktree-agent**: Workspace lifecycle agent. Asks the workspace maker (scripts/worktree.py, see docs/how-to/get-a-workspace.md) for the workspace of a feature branch or ticket, and asks the maker to clear a workspace after a branch merges. Getting a workspace is non-destructive (no confirmation required). Clearing one is destructive and requires an explicit "yes" after the safety-check report. Use when: user types /worktree; asks for a workspace for a branch or ticket; asks to clear or close a workspace after a PR merges. ([urlmonitor/leafcutter-ai](https://github.com/urlmonitor/leafcutter-ai/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/worktree-agent.md))
- **worktree-agent-a2b71cf252610c45b** ([prateekjain98/pyper](https://github.com/prateekjain98/pyper/blob/HEAD/coordination/agents/worktree-agent-a2b71cf252610c45b.md))
- **worktree-base-sync**: herdr の Workspace Fork(≒新規 worktree の作成)は親チェックアウトの HEAD を そのまま使い、fetch を挟まない。親を開いたまま何度も fork すると、新しい worktree が origin より何コミットも遅れた base から生まれる。既存の `pr-gate` はこれを SessionStart の advisory(「base 追従: origin/main から N コミット遅れています」)で伝えるが、**ブランチ自体は動かさない** — 履行歴を持つブランチを無断で動かすのは事故なので、これは意図した非対称 だった。 ([tarotene/dotfiles](https://github.com/tarotene/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree-base-sync.md) · ★1)
- **worktree-brief**: Paste this block into the brief of every sub-agent that works in its own worktree (`isolation: "worktree"`). ([fimoklei/maestro](https://github.com/fimoklei/maestro/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/worktree-brief.md) · ★1)

- **worktrees**: Claude Code sessions share one git working directory. - **wr-blog-article-critic**: Editorial-quality critic for windyroad blog article drafts (src/articles/&lt;slug&gt;/index.md). Reads the draft and the editorial-prompt rubric, returns a structured STRENGTHS + WEAKNESSES (+ optional RELEVANT CONTEXT) block with a computed VERDICT (PASS, WEAKNESSES_FOUND, PASS_WITH_AUTHOR_OVERRIDES, or REJECTED). Runs in a fresh context to avoid confirmation bias. Called in a 3-round iteration loop by the wr-blog skill (see ADR 033, ADR 035). ([windyroad/windyroad](https://github.com/windyroad/windyroad/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wr-blog-article-critic.md))
- **wr-blog-social-critic**: Editorial-quality critic for windyroad long-form social posts (LinkedIn, Reddit) generated from a published blog article. Reads the post and the editorial-prompt rubric, returns a structured STRENGTHS + WEAKNESSES (+ optional RELEVANT CONTEXT) block with a computed VERDICT (PASS, WEAKNESSES_FOUND, PASS_WITH_AUTHOR_OVERRIDES, or REJECTED). Runs in a fresh context to avoid confirmation bias. Called in an up-to-2-round iteration loop by the wr-blog:create-social-posts skill (see ADR 033, ADR 035). ([windyroad/windyroad](https://github.com/windyroad/windyroad/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wr-blog-social-critic.md))
- **wr-content-risk-scorer**: Content-risk scorer for AI-generated newsletter drafts. Reads the draft and the content-risk rubric, returns the five-axis CONTENT_RISK block with a computed VERDICT. Runs in a fresh context to avoid the confirmation bias of inline self-scoring. Output format pinned by ADR 015 confirmation criterion 3 and is consumed by SKILL.md step 16 save logic. See ADR 018. ([windyroad/windyroad](https://github.com/windyroad/windyroad/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wr-content-risk-scorer.md))
- **wr-newsletter-critic**: Editorial-quality critic for AI Engineering newsletter drafts (The Shift, Tokens Spent). Reads the draft and the editorial-prompt rubric, returns a structured STRENGTHS + WEAKNESSES (+ optional RELEVANT CONTEXT) block with a computed VERDICT (PASS, WEAKNESSES_FOUND, PASS_WITH_AUTHOR_OVERRIDES, or REJECTED). Runs in a fresh context to avoid confirmation bias. Called in a 3-round iteration loop by the wr-newsletter skill (see ADR 016, ADR 025, ADR 033, ADR 035). ([windyroad/windyroad](https://github.com/windyroad/windyroad/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wr-newsletter-critic.md))
- **wr-newsletter-cross-edition-consistency**: Cross-edition thesis-consistency gate for AI Engineering newsletter drafts (The Shift, Tokens Spent). Reads the current draft plus the prior N=8 published editions, returns a structured SUPPORTED / CONTRADICTS / NEUTRAL verdict with quoted passages on CONTRADICTS. Runs in a fresh context to avoid drafter anchoring. Called once per /wr-newsletter prep + finalise cycle at step 11.4 (between body draft at 11b and URL verification at 11.5) per ADR-038. Domain-specific naming per ADR-033. ([windyroad/windyroad](https://github.com/windyroad/windyroad/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wr-newsletter-cross-edition-consistency.md))
- **wr-newsletter-editor**: Editorial-sim reviewer for AI-generated newsletter drafts. Plays the role of an experienced LinkedIn newsletter editor and scores would-open / would-read-through / would-forward against the persona's documented JTBD, AND surfaces passage-cited editorial-craft weaknesses (opener-earns-thesis, fold-compression, audience-pointer-specificity, sentence-rhythm, atwn-thesis-fit, close-collects-the-so-what, signpost-promises-match-contents, item-placement, edition-internal-consistency) over the brief body. Returns an EDITOR_REVIEW block with a mechanical EDITOR_VERDICT.
